```python
@router.post("/calc/vertex-convert", summary="顶点距离换算")
async def calc_vertex_convert(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    payload: dict,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:view")),
):
    """按 Fc = Ff / (1 - d * Ff) 换算框架度数到隐形度数，可附带换算表。"""
    return ok(await svc.calc_vertex_convert(payload=payload))


# ==================== 散光片与配适评估 ====================


@router.post("/{fit_no}/toric/rotation", summary="提交散光片旋转测量与 LARS 补偿")
async def submit_toric_rotation(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    payload: dict,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:update")),
    fit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """校验旋转稳定性并按 LARS 法则换算下单轴位（旋转 > 10° 强制补偿）。"""
    return ok(await svc.submit_toric_rotation(fit_no=fit_no, payload=payload, operator=user))


@router.post("/{fit_no}/adaptation", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
             summary="新增配适评估记录")
async def add_adaptation(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    payload: AdaptationEvalIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:update")),
    fit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """追加一条 append-only 的配适评估记录，返回 RGP 逼近建议。"""
    return ok(await svc.add_adaptation(fit_no=fit_no, payload=payload, operator=user))


@router.get("/{fit_no}/adaptation", summary="配适评估记录列表")
async def list_adaptation(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:view")),
    fit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    eye_side: Optional[Literal["OD", "OS"]] = Query(None),
    eval_stage: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    with_photos: bool = Query(False),
):
    """按眼别与阶段返回评估链，附旋转度与舒适度趋势。"""
    return ok(await svc.list_adaptation(fit_no=fit_no, eye_side=eye_side,
                                        eval_stage=eval_stage, with_photos=with_photos))


# ==================== 状态流转 ====================


@router.post("/{fit_no}/submit", summary="提交验配申请")
async def submit_fitting(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    payload: dict,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:update")),
    fit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """执行六项前置校验并把草稿推进为 PENDING_TRIAL 或 TRIAL。"""
    return ok(await svc.submit(fit_no=fit_no, payload=payload, operator=user))


@router.post("/{fit_no}/fit-complete", summary="完成验配")
async def fit_complete(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    payload: FittingFitCompleteIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:update")),
    fit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """校验试戴片归还、签名与知情同意后把 TRIAL 推进为 FITTED。"""
    return ok(await svc.fit_complete(fit_no=fit_no, payload=payload, operator=user))


@router.post("/{fit_no}/approve", summary="审核批准")
async def approve_fitting(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    payload: FittingApproveIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:audit")),
    fit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """审核医师批准验配单：禁忌、知情同意、处方、自审、试戴归还五项前置校验。"""
    result = await svc.approve(fit_no=fit_no, payload=payload, operator=user)
    return JSONResponse(content={"code": 0, "message": "success",
                                 "data": result.model_dump(mode="json"),
                                 "trace_id": user.trace_id})


@router.post("/{fit_no}/reject", summary="审核驳回")
async def reject_fitting(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    payload: FittingRejectIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:audit")),
    fit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """审核医师驳回验配单，驳回原因不少于 5 字。"""
    return ok(await svc.reject(fit_no=fit_no, payload=payload, operator=user))


@router.post("/{fit_no}/cancel", summary="作废验配单")
async def cancel_fitting(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    payload: FittingCancelIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:update")),
    fit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """作废验配单：已下单不可作废，试戴片未归还不可作废。"""
    return ok(await svc.cancel(fit_no=fit_no, payload=payload, operator=user))


# ==================== 试戴片管理 ====================


@router.get("/trial", summary="试戴片借用台账")
async def list_trial_use(
    svc: TrialSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:trial")),
    trial_status: Literal["ALL", "OPEN", "RETURNED", "OVERDUE",
                          "PENDING_DISINFECTION", "SCRAPPED"] = Query("ALL"),
    product_code: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32),
    trial_serial_no: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
):
    """按状态、产品、序列号查询试戴片借出台账，附库存概览。"""
    return ok(await svc.list_trial(
        status=trial_status, product_code=product_code,
        trial_serial_no=trial_serial_no, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        org_ids=user.org_ids))


@router.post("/trial/borrow", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED, summary="试戴片借出登记")
async def borrow_trial(
    svc: TrialSvc,
    payload: TrialBorrowIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:trial")),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
):
    """一片一码借出登记，唯一部分索引保证一片同时只被一个患者占用。"""
    return ok(await svc.borrow(payload=payload, operator=user,
                               idempotency_key=idempotency_key))


@router.post("/trial/return", summary="试戴片归还核对")
async def return_trial(
    svc: TrialSvc,
    payload: TrialReturnIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:trial")),
):
    """归还核对：完好/缺陷/丢失，归还后消毒状态重置为待消毒。"""
    return ok(await svc.return_lens(payload=payload, operator=user))


@router.post("/trial/disinfect", summary="试戴片消毒登记")
async def disinfect_trial(
    svc: TrialSvc,
    payload: TrialDisinfectIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:trial")),
):
    """登记消毒方法、时长与护理液批号，时长不足配置下限时拒绝。"""
    return ok(await svc.disinfect(payload=payload, operator=user))


# ==================== 护理计划 ====================


@router.post("/{fit_no}/care-plan", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
             summary="创建护理指导与随访计划")
async def create_care_plan(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    payload: CarePlanCreateIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:care")),
    fit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """创建护理计划并推送复查节点至 M10-09。"""
    return ok(await svc.create_care_plan(fit_no=fit_no, payload=payload, operator=user))


@router.put("/care-plan/{plan_no}", summary="更新或终止护理计划")
async def update_care_plan(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    payload: dict,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:care")),
    plan_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """更新戴镜时长、复查节点、风险等级；plan_status=TERMINATED 时终止计划。"""
    return ok(await svc.update_care_plan(plan_no=plan_no, payload=payload, operator=user))


# ==================== 统计 ====================


@router.get("/statistics", summary="验配统计与质量指标")
async def statistics(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:stat")),
    date_from: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    date_to: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
):
    """返回卡片、趋势、类型结构、旋转分布、试戴片与复查指标。"""
    data = await svc.statistics(date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to,
                                org_id=org_id, org_ids=user.org_ids)
    return ok(data, headers={"Cache-Control": "private, max-age=60"})


@router.get("/statistics/by-fitter", summary="验配师工作量与质量排名")
async def statistics_by_fitter(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:stat")),
    date_from: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    date_to: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    sort: Literal["fit_total", "success_rate", "avg_fit_minutes",
                  "complication_count"] = Query("fit_total"),
    order: Literal["asc", "desc"] = Query("desc"),
    limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
):
    """按验配师聚合工作量与质量指标并排名。"""
    return ok(await svc.statistics_by_fitter(
        date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to, org_id=org_id,
        sort=sort, order=order, limit=limit, org_ids=user.org_ids))


# ==================== 配置 ====================


@router.get("/config", summary="查询验配规则与参数配置")
async def get_config(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:view")),
    group: Optional[Literal["FITTING", "TRIAL", "CARE", "FOLLOWUP",
                            "PRESCRIPTION"]] = Query(None),
    scope: Literal["GLOBAL", "BRAND", "ORG"] = Query("GLOBAL"),
    scope_value: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
):
    """返回参数列表与按 ORG > BRAND > GLOBAL 计算的生效值映射。"""
    return ok(await svc.get_config(group=group, scope=scope, scope_value=scope_value))


@router.put("/config", summary="修改验配规则与参数配置")
async def update_config(
    svc: FittingSvc,
    items: list[ConfigUpdateIn],
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m10:contact:config")),
):
    """批量修改参数，逐条校验类型、范围与乐观锁，成功后失效缓存。"""
    return ok(await svc.update_config(items=items, operator=user))
```

```python
# app/api/internal/m10/contact.py
"""M10-03 隐形眼镜验配 —— 内部契约路由（P-08）

路径与共享规范 §8 一字不差：
    GET /internal/v1/m10/contact-fitting/{fit_no}
"""

from typing import Annotated, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Path, Query

from app.api.deps import DbSession, ServiceAuth
from app.common.response import ok
from app.services.m10.contact_service import ContactFittingService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m10/contact-fitting", tags=["M10-03 内部契约"])


def get_fitting_service(db: DbSession) -> ContactFittingService:
    """验配单服务依赖（内部契约复用同一 Service）。"""
    return ContactFittingService(db)


@router.get("/{fit_no}", summary="P-08 取隐形验配单")
async def get_fitting_contract(
    svc: Annotated[ContactFittingService, Depends(get_fitting_service)],
    fit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32, description="验配单号 CF+YYYYMMDD+4 位"),
    auth: ServiceAuth = Depends(),
):
    """供 M16-04（收钱前合规校验）与 M10-06（下单取参数）调用。

    - 两条 SQL 完成查询（主表 + 眼别明细 is_final=TRUE），P95 ≤ 100ms
    - 已作废/已删除返回 "1003001"
    - rx_valid 由服务端按当日实时计算，不缓存过夜
    """
    data = await svc.get_contract(fit_no=fit_no, caller=auth.service_name)
    return ok(data, headers={"Cache-Control": "private, max-age=60"})


@router.get("/by-patient/{patient_id}/history", summary="患者隐形验配历史")
async def get_patient_history(
    svc: Annotated[ContactFittingService, Depends(get_fitting_service)],
    patient_id: int = Path(..., gt=0, description="患者 ID"),
    auth: ServiceAuth = Depends(),
    limit: int = Query(5, ge=1, le=20),
    only_approved: bool = Query(True, description="仅已批准且处方有效的单"),
):
    """供 M10-06 复购/补配参考历史参数，供 M10-09 展示隐形配戴史。"""
    data = await svc.get_patient_history(
        patient_id=patient_id, limit=limit, only_approved=only_approved,
        caller=auth.service_name)
    return ok(data, headers={"Cache-Control": "private, max-age=300"})


# 注意：静态路径 /by-patient/... 必须在 /{fit_no} 之前注册，
# 否则会被 /{fit_no} 捕获。此处通过 include_router 的顺序保证（见 main.py）。
```

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m10/contact_service.py
"""M10-03 隐形眼镜验配 —— 核心业务服务

本文件承载子模块最关键的三个算法与校验：
  1. 验配单详情组装（P-08 契约的数据源）
  2. LARS 散光轴位等效换算
  3. 顶点距离换算 + 六项提交前置校验
"""

import json
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, or_, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m10.m01_optical_client import M01OpticalClient
from app.clients.m10.m03_patient_client import M03PatientClient
from app.clients.m10.m10_prescription_client import M10PrescriptionClient
from app.clients.m10.m15_stock_client import M15StockClient
from app.common.errors import BizError
from app.common.idempotency import IdempotencyStore
from app.common.redis import RedisPool
from app.models.m10.contact import (
    M10ContactAdaptationLog,
    M10ContactCarePlan,
    M10ContactConfig,
    M10ContactFitting,
    M10ContactFittingEye,
    M10ContactLog,
    M10ContactTrialUse,
)
from app.schemas.m10.contact import (
    FittingApproveIn,
    FittingCancelIn,
    FittingCreateIn,
    FittingFitCompleteIn,
    FittingRejectIn,
    FittingUpdateIn,
)
from app.services.m10.contact_algorithms import (
    ContactCalculators,
    KeratometryResult,
    ScreeningResult,
)
from app.services.m10.contact_rules import (
    ANTIBIOTIC,
    CONTRA_BUT_SECONDS,
    CONTRA_ENDOTHELIAL,
    CONTRA_SCHIRMER_MM,
    DEFAULT_FOLLOWUP_NODES,
    VERTEX_THRESHOLD,
    WARN_BUT_SECONDS,
)
from app.utils.codegen import next_biz_no
from app.utils.audit import write_audit

D = Decimal


class ContactFittingService:
    """隐形眼镜验配单核心服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.optical = M01OpticalClient()
        self.patient = M03PatientClient()
        self.prescription = M10PrescriptionClient()
        self.stock = M15StockClient()
        self.redis = RedisPool.get()
        self.algorithms = ContactCalculators(config_loader=self._load_config)

    # ==================== 配置 ====================

    async def _load_config(self, key: str, org_id: int = 1) -> Any:
        """按 ORG > BRAND > GLOBAL 优先级加载配置项（带 Redis 缓存）。"""
        cache_key = f"m10:contact:config:eff:{org_id}:{key}"
        cached = await self.redis.get(cache_key)
        if cached is not None:
            return json.loads(cached)

        stmt = (
            select(M10ContactConfig)
            .where(
                M10ContactConfig.config_key == key,
                M10ContactConfig.status == "ENABLED",
                M10ContactConfig.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
            .order_by(
                # ORG(0) > BRAND(1) > GLOBAL(2)
                func.coalesce(
                    func.nullif(M10ContactConfig.scope, "GLOBAL"), "ZZZ"
                ).asc()
            )
        )
        rows = (await self.db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()
        if not rows:
            return None

        ordered = sorted(
            rows,
            key=lambda r: (
                {"ORG": 0, "BRAND": 1, "GLOBAL": 2}[r.scope],
                0 if r.scope_value == str(org_id) else 1,
            ),
        )
        value = ordered[0].parsed_value()
        await self.redis.set(cache_key, json.dumps(value), ex=300)
        return value

    # ==================== P-08 契约：详情与契约出参 ====================

    async def get_detail(
        self,
        fit_no: str,
        reveal_phone: bool = False,
        operator: Optional[Any] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """验配单详情：主表 + 眼别明细 + 试戴片 + 护理计划 + 轨迹，禁止 N+1。"""
        fitting = await self._get_fitting(fit_no)
        eyes = await self._load_eyes(fitting.id)
        trial_uses = await self._load_open_trials(fitting.id)
        care_plan = await self._load_active_care_plan(fitting.id)
        logs = await self._load_recent_logs(fitting.id, limit=20)

        detail = self._assemble_detail(fitting, eyes, trial_uses, care_plan, logs)
        if reveal_phone and operator is not None:
            detail["phone"] = fitting.phone
            await write_audit(
                self.db,
                action="VIEW",
                object_type="FITTING",
                object_id=fitting.id,
                operator_id=operator.staff_id,
                reason="REVEAL_PHONE",
            )
        return detail

    async def get_contract(self, fit_no: str, caller: str = "UNKNOWN") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """P-08：供 M16-04 / M10-06 消费的最精简出参（P95 ≤ 100ms）。"""
        fitting = await self._get_fitting(fit_no, allow_cancelled=False)

        # 缓存（写操作会主动删除该键）
        cache_key = f"m10:contact:contract:{fit_no}"
        cached = await self.redis.get(cache_key)
        if cached is not None:
            return json.loads(cached)

        eyes = await self._load_eyes(fitting.id, final_only=True)
        rx_valid = self._calc_rx_valid(fitting)

        data: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "fit_no": fitting.fit_no,
            "patient_id": fitting.patient_id,
            "patient_name": fitting.patient_name,
            "org_id": fitting.org_id,
            "fitting_type": fitting.fitting_type,
            "lens_category": fitting.lens_category,
            "status": fitting.status,
            "product_code": fitting.product_code,
            "product_name": fitting.product_name,
            "brand_name": fitting.brand_name,
            "replacement_cycle": fitting.replacement_cycle,
            "supply_type": fitting.supply_type,
            "is_toric": fitting.is_toric,
            "is_trial_lens": False,
            "rx_no": fitting.rx_no,
            "rx_valid": rx_valid,
            "rx_valid_until": self._iso(fitting.rx_valid_until),
            "rx_source": fitting.prescription_source,
            "fitting_result": fitting.fitting_result,
            "contraindication_hit": fitting.contraindication_hit,
            "compliance_ack": fitting.compliance_ack,
            "care_plan_no": None,
            "next_followup_date": None,
            "eyes": [self._eye_contract_out(e) for e in eyes],
            "order_no": fitting.order_no,
            "sales_order_no": fitting.sales_order_no,
            "fitted_at": self._iso(fitting.fitted_at),
            "approved_at": self._iso(fitting.approved_at),
        }

        care_plan = await self._load_active_care_plan(fitting.id)
        if care_plan is not None:
            data["care_plan_no"] = care_plan.plan_no
            data["next_followup_date"] = self._iso(care_plan.next_followup_date)

        # 试戴片不得作为销售品出现在契约里
        if fitting.product_code and await self._is_trial_product(fitting.product_code):
            raise BizError("1003027", "该产品为试戴片，不可销售")

        await self.redis.set(cache_key, json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False), ex=60)
        await self._log_contract_access(fitting, caller)
        return data

    # ==================== 建单 ====================

    async def create(
        self,
        payload: FittingCreateIn,
        operator: Any,
        idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """新建验配申请：患者快照 + 凭处方校验 + 产品合规 + 重复开单防护。"""
        idem = IdempotencyStore(self.redis, biz="contact", key=idempotency_key)
        if await idem.exists():
            return await idem.load()

        # 1) 患者快照（U-06，阻断）
        patient = await self.patient.brief(payload.patient_id)
        if not patient:
            raise BizError("1003004", f"患者 {payload.patient_id} 不存在")

        # 2) 凭处方校验（隐形眼镜必须凭处方销售）
        rx_valid_until = await self._validate_prescription(payload)

        # 3) 验光单归属校验（U-04）
        if payload.optometry_no:
            await self._assert_optometry_belongs(payload.optometry_no, payload.patient_id)

        # 4) 产品合规（U-01）：非试戴片、注册证有效、类型匹配
        await self._validate_product(
            product_code=payload.product_code,
            trial_code=payload.trial_product_code,
            fitting_type=payload.fitting_type,
            lens_category=payload.lens_category,
        )

        # 5) 重复开单防护
        existing = await self._find_active_fitting(payload.patient_id, payload.org_id)
        if existing is not None:
            raise BizError(
                "1003032",
                f"该患者存在进行中的隐形验配单 {existing.fit_no or existing.id}",
                data={"fit_no": existing.fit_no, "id": existing.id},
            )

        # 6) 禁忌预检（不阻断建单，仅预置标记）
        screening = self.algorithms.evaluate_screening(
            but_seconds=payload.but_seconds,
            schirmer_mm=payload.schirmer_1_mm,
            endo_od=payload.endothelial_density_od,
            endo_os=payload.endothelial_density_os,
            fissure_od=payload.palpebral_fissure_od,
            fissure_os=payload.palpebral_fissure_os,
            eyelid=payload.eyelid_condition,
            has_keratitis=payload.has_keratitis,
        )

        fitting = M10ContactFitting(
            fit_no=None,
            patient_id=payload.patient_id,
            patient_name=patient["name"],
            gender=patient.get("gender"),
            birth_date=patient.get("birth_date"),
            age=patient.get("age"),
            phone=patient.get("phone"),
            empi_no=patient.get("empi_no"),
            customer_id=payload.customer_id,
            org_id=payload.org_id,
            rx_no=payload.rx_no,
            optometry_no=payload.optometry_no,
            prescription_source=payload.prescription_source,
            external_rx_no=payload.external_rx_no,
            external_rx_date=payload.external_rx_date,
            rx_valid_until=rx_valid_until,
            fitting_type=payload.fitting_type,
            lens_category=payload.lens_category,
            eye_scope=payload.eye_scope,
            visit_type=payload.visit_type,
            product_code=payload.product_code,
            trial_product_code=payload.trial_product_code,
            but_seconds=payload.but_seconds,
            schirmer_1_mm=payload.schirmer_1_mm,
            endothelial_density_od=payload.endothelial_density_od,
            endothelial_density_os=payload.endothelial_density_os,
            palpebral_fissure_od=payload.palpebral_fissure_od,
            palpebral_fissure_os=payload.palpebral_fissure_os,
            slit_lamp_od=payload.slit_lamp_od,
            slit_lamp_os=payload.slit_lamp_os,
            eyelid_condition=payload.eyelid_condition,
            has_dry_eye=payload.has_dry_eye,
            has_keratitis=payload.has_keratitis,
            contraindication_hit=screening.contraindication_hit,
            contraindication_codes=screening.contraindication_codes or None,
            screening_summary=screening.to_dict(),
            optometrist_id=payload.optometrist_id,
            fitter_id=payload.fitter_id,
            status="DRAFT",
            remark=payload.remark,
            created_by=operator.staff_id,
        )
        self.db.add(fitting)
        await self.db.flush()

        await write_audit(
            self.db, action="CREATE", object_type="FITTING",
            object_id=fitting.id, operator_id=operator.staff_id)

        result = {
            "id": fitting.id,
            "fit_no": None,
            "status": "DRAFT",
            "version": 0,
            "next_action": "SAVE_EYES",
            "rx_valid_until": self._iso(rx_valid_until),
            "warnings": screening.warnings,
        }
        await self.db.commit()
        await idem.save(result)
        return result

    # ==================== 完成验配与审核批准 ====================

    async def fit_complete(
        self,
        fit_no: str,
        payload: FittingFitCompleteIn,
        operator: Any,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """TRIAL → FITTED：结论 + 签名 + 试戴片归还 + 知情同意 + 算法完整性。"""
        fitting = await self._get_fitting(fit_no)
        self._assert_status(fitting, {"TRIAL"})
        self._assert_version(fitting, payload.version)

        if not payload.fitting_result:
            raise BizError("1003017", "验配结论未填写")
        if payload.fitting_result in ("FIT_FAILED", "REFER_FURTHER"):
            if not payload.conclusion_note or len(payload.conclusion_note.strip()) < 10:
                raise BizError("1003003", "验配失败或转诊必须填写不少于 10 字的结论说明")
        if not payload.signature_url:
            raise BizError("1003017", "验配师未签名")
        if not payload.compliance_ack:
            raise BizError("1003012", "患者未签署知情同意书")

        # 试戴片必须全部归还
        if fitting.trial_needed and not fitting.trial_returned:
            open_trials = await self._load_open_trials(fitting.id)
            raise BizError(
                "1003016",
                "试戴片尚未全部归还，不能完成验配",
                data={"open_trials": [t.trial_no for t in open_trials]},
            )

        # 算法完整性复核（防绕过前端）
        eyes = await self._load_eyes(fitting.id, final_only=True)
        self._assert_algorithm_integrity(eyes)

        # 双眼结论完整性
        if fitting.eye_scope == "OU":
            if len(eyes) < 2:
                raise BizError("1003003", "双眼验配必须完成两眼配适评估")
            if any(e.fit_conclusion == "FAIL" for e in eyes):
                if payload.fitting_result == "FIT_SUCCESS":
                    raise BizError("1003003", "存在配适不通过的眼别，结论不能为验配成功")

        from_status = fitting.status
        fitting.status = "FITTED"
        fitting.fitting_result = payload.fitting_result
        fitting.conclusion_note = payload.conclusion_note
        fitting.signature_url = payload.signature_url
        fitting.compliance_ack = payload.compliance_ack
        fitting.fitted_at = datetime.now()
        fitting.version += 1
        fitting.updated_by = operator.staff_id

        await write_audit(
            self.db, action="FIT_COMPLETE", object_type="FITTING",
            object_id=fitting.id, operator_id=operator.staff_id,
            from_status=from_status, to_status="FITTED")
        await self.db.commit()
        await self._invalidate_contract_cache(fit_no)

        return {
            "fit_no": fitting.fit_no,
            "status": fitting.status,
            "from_status": from_status,
            "fitting_result": fitting.fitting_result,
            "fitted_at": self._iso(fitting.fitted_at),
            "next_action": "PENDING_AUDIT",
            "audit_required": True,
        }

    async def approve(
        self,
        fit_no: str,
        payload: FittingApproveIn,
        operator: Any,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """FITTED → APPROVED：五项前置校验 + 算法完整性 + 处方复核。"""
        fitting = await self._get_fitting(fit_no)
        self._assert_status(fitting, {"FITTED"})
        self._assert_version(fitting, payload.version)

        # ① 禁忌症
        if fitting.contraindication_hit:
            raise BizError(
                "1003008",
                "命中隐形眼镜禁忌症，不允许批准",
                data={"contraindication_codes": fitting.contraindication_codes or []},
            )
        # ② 知情同意
        if not fitting.compliance_ack:
            raise BizError("1003012", "患者未签署知情同意书")
        # ③ 处方有效性（不信任本地快照，实时复核）
        if fitting.rx_no:
            rx = await self.prescription.get(fitting.rx_no)
            if not rx:
                raise BizError("1003002", "配镜处方不存在，隐形眼镜必须凭处方验配")
            if rx.get("status") != "AUDITED":
                raise BizError("1003002", f"处方状态为 {rx.get('status')}，不可用于验配")
            if rx.get("valid_until") and rx["valid_until"] < date.today():
                raise BizError("1003009", f"处方已过期，有效期至 {rx['valid_until']}")
        elif fitting.prescription_source != "EXTERNAL":
            raise BizError("1003002", "缺少配镜处方号，隐形眼镜必须凭处方验配")
        # ④ 不得自审
        if fitting.fitter_id and payload.audit_doctor_id == fitting.fitter_id:
            raise BizError("1003019", "验配师与审核医师不得为同一人")
        # ⑤ 试戴片已归还
        if fitting.trial_needed and not fitting.trial_returned:
            raise BizError("1003016", "试戴片尚未全部归还，不允许批准")

        eyes = await self._load_eyes(fitting.id, final_only=True)
        self._assert_algorithm_integrity(eyes)
        if any(e.fit_conclusion == "FAIL" for e in eyes):
            raise BizError("1003014", "存在配适不通过的眼别，不允许批准")

        # 处方有效天数覆盖（只允许缩短，延长需额外权限）
        if payload.rx_valid_days:
            new_until = date.today() + timedelta(days=payload.rx_valid_days)
            if fitting.rx_valid_until and new_until > fitting.rx_valid_until:
                if not operator.has_perm("m10:contact:audit_extend"):
                    raise BizError("1003003", "延长处方有效期需 m10:contact:audit_extend 权限")
            fitting.rx_valid_until = new_until

        from_status = fitting.status
        fitting.status = "APPROVED"
        fitting.audit_doctor_id = payload.audit_doctor_id
        fitting.audit_doctor_name = await self._staff_name(payload.audit_doctor_id)
        fitting.approved_at = datetime.now()
        fitting.version += 1

        await write_audit(
            self.db, action="APPROVE", object_type="FITTING",
            object_id=fitting.id, operator_id=operator.staff_id,
            from_status=from_status, to_status="APPROVED",
            reason=payload.approve_note)
        await self.db.commit()
        await self._invalidate_contract_cache(fit_no)
        await self._publish_fitting_created(fitting, eyes)

        return {
            "fit_no": fitting.fit_no,
            "status": fitting.status,
            "from_status": from_status,
            "audit_doctor_id": fitting.audit_doctor_id,
            "audit_doctor_name": fitting.audit_doctor_name,
            "approved_at": self._iso(fitting.approved_at),
            "rx_valid_until": self._iso(fitting.rx_valid_until),
            "printable": True,
            "orderable": True,
        }

    # ==================== 内部算法与校验 ====================

    def _assert_algorithm_integrity(self, eyes: List[M10ContactFittingEye]) -> None:
        """算法完整性复核：旋转 > 10° 必须补偿，超 4.00D 必须顶点换算。"""
        for eye in eyes:
            rotation = eye.rotation_degree or 0
            if abs(rotation) > 10 and not eye.axis_compensation_applied:
                raise BizError(
                    "1003013",
                    f"{eye.eye_side} 镜片旋转 {rotation}° 超过 10°，必须先做 LARS 轴位等效换算",
                    data={"eye_side": eye.eye_side, "rotation_degree": rotation},
                )
            frame = eye.frame_sphere
            if frame is not None and abs(frame) > VERTEX_THRESHOLD:
                if not eye.vertex_convert_applied:
                    raise BizError(
                        "1003024",
                        f"{eye.eye_side} 框架度数 {frame}D 超过 4.00D，必须做顶点距离换算",
                        data={"eye_side": eye.eye_side, "frame_sphere": str(frame)},
                    )

    async def _validate_prescription(self, payload: FittingCreateIn) -> date:
        """凭处方校验并推算处方有效期（3.4.9）。"""
        if payload.prescription_source == "EXTERNAL":
            if not payload.external_rx_no or not payload.external_rx_date:
                raise BizError("1003011", "外院处方必须填写外院处方号与开具日期")
            external_days = await self._load_config(
                "PRESCRIPTION.VALID_DAYS.EXTERNAL", payload.org_id) or 30
            valid_until = payload.external_rx_date + timedelta(days=int(external_days))
            if payload.external_rx_date > date.today():
                raise BizError("1003011", "外院处方开具日期不能晚于今日")
            if date.today() > valid_until:
                raise BizError(
                    "1003009",
                    f"外院处方已超过 {external_days} 天有效期（{payload.external_rx_date}）")
            return valid_until

        if not payload.rx_no:
            raise BizError("1003002", "隐形眼镜必须凭处方验配，请先开立配镜处方")

        rx = await self.prescription.get(payload.rx_no)
        if not rx:
            raise BizError("1003002", f"配镜处方 {payload.rx_no} 不存在")
        if rx.get("status") != "AUDITED":
            raise BizError("1003002", f"处方状态为 {rx.get('status')}，需审核通过后方可用于验配")
        if rx.get("valid_until") and rx["valid_until"] < date.today():
            raise BizError("1003009", f"处方已过期，有效期至 {rx['valid_until']}")

        if payload.fitting_type == "RGP":
            key = "PRESCRIPTION.VALID_DAYS.RGP"
            default_days = 180
        else:
            key = "PRESCRIPTION.VALID_DAYS.SOFT"
            default_days = 365
        days = await self._load_config(key, payload.org_id) or default_days
        return date.today() + timedelta(days=int(days))

    async def _validate_product(
        self,
        product_code: Optional[str],
        trial_code: Optional[str],
        fitting_type: str,
        lens_category: str,
    ) -> None:
        """产品合规：非试戴片可售、注册证有效、类型与镜片设计匹配。"""
        if not product_code:
            return
        cfg = await self.optical.full_config(product_code)
        if not cfg:
            raise BizError("1003006", f"产品 {product_code} 不存在或已停用")
        product = cfg.get("product", {})
        if product.get("is_trial_lens"):
            raise BizError("1003027", "试戴片不可作为销售产品，请选择正式销售产品")
        reg_no = product.get("registration_no")
        reg_exp = product.get("registration_expire")
        if not reg_no:
            raise BizError("1003023", "隐形眼镜为三类医疗器械，必须维护注册证号")
        if reg_exp and date.fromisoformat(str(reg_exp)) < date.today():
            raise BizError("1003023", f"医疗器械注册证已于 {reg_exp} 过期")

        if fitting_type == "TORIC" and lens_category not in ("TORIC", "TORIC_RGP"):
            raise BizError("1003003", "散光软镜验配的镜片设计类别必须为 TORIC")
        if product.get("is_toric") and fitting_type not in ("TORIC", "MULTIFOCAL"):
            raise BizError("1003003", "所选产品为散光片，验配类型必须为 TORIC 或 MULTIFOCAL")

        if trial_code:
            if trial_code == product_code:
                raise BizError("1003027", "试戴片产品编码不能与销售产品编码相同")
            trial_cfg = await self.optical.full_config(trial_code)
            if not trial_cfg or not trial_cfg.get("product", {}).get("is_trial_lens"):
                raise BizError("1003027", f"产品 {trial_code} 不是试戴片（is_trial_lens=false）")

    # ==================== 辅助 ====================

    async def _get_fitting(self, fit_no: str, allow_cancelled: bool = True) -> M10ContactFitting:
        """按验配单号取主表，不存在/已删除/已作废时按需报错。"""
        stmt = select(M10ContactFitting).where(
            M10ContactFitting.fit_no == fit_no,
            M10ContactFitting.is_deleted.is_(False),
        )
        fitting = (await self.db.execute(stmt)).scalar_one_or_none()
        if fitting is None:
            raise BizError("1003001", f"验配单 {fit_no} 不存在或已删除")
        if not allow_cancelled and fitting.status == "CANCELLED":
            raise BizError("1003001", f"验配单 {fit_no} 已作废")
        return fitting

    async def _load_eyes(
        self, fitting_id: int, final_only: bool = False
    ) -> List[M10ContactFittingEye]:
        """取验配单眼别明细（命中 idx_m10_contact_fitting_eye_fit）。"""
        stmt = select(M10ContactFittingEye).where(
            M10ContactFittingEye.fitting_id == fitting_id,
            M10ContactFittingEye.is_deleted.is_(False),
        )
        if final_only:
            stmt = stmt.where(M10ContactFittingEye.is_final.is_(True))
        stmt = stmt.order_by(M10ContactFittingEye.eye_side, M10ContactFittingEye.seq_no)
        return list((await self.db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all())

    def _calc_rx_valid(self, fitting: M10ContactFitting) -> bool:
        """处方是否仍在有效期内（含当日，服务端实时计算不缓存过夜）。"""
        if fitting.rx_valid_until is None:
            return False
        return fitting.rx_valid_until >= date.today()

    def _assert_status(self, fitting: M10ContactFitting, allowed: set) -> None:
        """状态机前置校验。"""
        if fitting.status not in allowed:
            raise BizError(
                "1003031",
                f"当前状态 {fitting.status} 不允许该操作（允许：{'/'.join(sorted(allowed))}）")

    def _assert_version(self, fitting: M10ContactFitting, version: int) -> None:
        """乐观锁校验。"""
        if fitting.version != version:
            raise BizError("1003038", "单据已被他人修改，请刷新后重试")

    async def _invalidate_contract_cache(self, fit_no: str) -> None:
        """验配单写操作后失效契约缓存。"""
        await self.redis.delete(f"m10:contact:contract:{fit_no}")

    @staticmethod
    def _iso(value: Optional[datetime | date]) -> Optional[str]:
        """转 ISO 8601 字符串（带时区）。"""
        return value.isoformat() if value else None

    @staticmethod
    def _eye_contract_out(eye: M10ContactFittingEye) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """眼别明细 → 契约出参（下单参数全部为已换算/已补偿后的值）。"""
        return {
            "eye_side": eye.eye_side,
            "sphere": float(eye.sphere) if eye.sphere is not None else None,
            "cylinder": float(eye.cylinder) if eye.cylinder is not None else None,
            "axis": eye.axis,
            "base_curve": float(eye.base_curve) if eye.base_curve is not None else None,
            "diameter": float(eye.diameter) if eye.diameter is not None else None,
            "lens_sphere_final": (float(eye.lens_sphere_final)
                                  if eye.lens_sphere_final is not None else None),
            "vertex_convert_applied": eye.vertex_convert_applied,
            "rotation_degree": eye.rotation_degree,
            "compensated_axis": eye.compensated_axis,
            "axis_compensation_applied": eye.axis_compensation_applied,
            "replacement_cycle": eye.replacement_cycle,
            "fit_grade": eye.fit_grade,
            "fit_conclusion": eye.fit_conclusion,
        }
```

```python
# app/services/m10/contact_algorithms.py
"""M10-03 隐形眼镜验配 —— 纯算法模块（无 IO，可单测）

包含三大核心算法：
  1. 角膜曲率 → 基弧 / 直径推荐
  2. 顶点距离换算（框架度数 → 隐形度数）
  3. LARS 散光轴位等效换算
四、禁忌症分级判定
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional

D = Decimal
STEP = D("0.25")
VERTEX_THRESHOLD = D("4.00")


@dataclass
class KeratometryResult:
    """角膜曲率换算结果。"""

    k_avg: D
    k_avg_mm: D
    corneal_astigmatism: D
    astigmatism_type: str

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """序列化为契约出参。"""
        return {
            "k_avg": float(self.k_avg),
            "k_avg_mm": float(self.k_avg_mm),
            "corneal_astigmatism": float(self.corneal_astigmatism),
            "astigmatism_type": self.astigmatism_type,
        }


@dataclass
class ScreeningResult:
    """禁忌症筛查结果。"""

    contraindication_hit: bool = False
    contraindication_codes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: List[Dict[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)
    risk_level: str = "LOW"
    risk_factors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """序列化为 screening_summary JSONB。"""
        return {
            "contraindication_hit": self.contraindication_hit,
            "contraindication_codes": self.contraindication_codes,
            "items": self.items,
            "risk_level": self.risk_level,
            "risk_factors": self.risk_factors,
        }


class ContactCalculators:
    """隐形眼镜验配核心算法集合（纯函数，便于单元测试）。"""

    def __init__(self, config_loader: Optional[Callable] = None) -> None:
        self._load_config = config_loader

    # ==================== 1. 角膜曲率 ====================

    @staticmethod
    def keratometry(k1: D, k2: D, k2_axis: Optional[int] = None) -> KeratometryResult:
        """由 K1（平）/ K2（陡）计算平均曲率、曲率半径、角膜散光与散光类型。"""
        k1, k2 = D(str(k1)), D(str(k2))
        k_avg = ((k1 + k2) / D(2)).quantize(D("0.01"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        k_avg_mm = (D("337.5") / k_avg).quantize(D("0.01"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        astig = abs(k2 - k1).quantize(D("0.01"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

        astig_type = "OBLIQUE"
        if k2_axis is not None:
            if 60 <= k2_axis <= 120:
                astig_type = "WTR"
            elif k2_axis <= 30 or k2_axis >= 150:
                astig_type = "ATR"

        return KeratometryResult(
            k_avg=k_avg, k_avg_mm=k_avg_mm,
            corneal_astigmatism=astig, astigmatism_type=astig_type,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def snap_to_option(value: D, options: List[D], prefer_flatter: bool = True) -> Optional[D]:
        """吸附到产品可供档位：就近，等距时取更平坦（更大）档位。"""
        if not options:
            return None
        equal = [o for o in options if o == value]
        if equal:
            return equal[0]
        distances = sorted(((abs(o - value), -o if prefer_flatter else o, o) for o in options))
        return distances[0][2]

    def recommend_base_curve(
        self,
        keratometry: KeratometryResult,
        flat_factor: D = D("0.8"),
        options: Optional[List[D]] = None,
        min_deviation: D = D("0.6"),
        max_deviation: D = D("1.0"),
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """软镜基弧推荐（3.4.2）：BC = 平均曲率半径 + 平坦化系数。"""
        recommended = (keratometry.k_avg_mm + D(str(flat_factor))).quantize(D("0.01"))
        snapped = self.snap_to_option(recommended, options or []) or recommended
        deviation = snapped - keratometry.k_avg_mm

        if deviation < min_deviation:
            flag = "STEEP"
        elif deviation > max_deviation:
            flag = "FLAT"
        else:
            flag = "OK"
        if options and snapped not in options:
            flag = "OUT_OF_RANGE"

        return {
            "recommended_mm": float(recommended),
            "snapped_mm": float(snapped),
            "deviation_mm": float(deviation.quantize(D("0.01"))),
            "deviation_flag": flag,
            "available_options": [float(o) for o in (options or [])],
            "formula": f"{keratometry.k_avg_mm} + {flat_factor} = {recommended} → 吸附 {snapped}",
        }

    def recommend_diameter(
        self,
        hvid: D,
        offset: D = D("1.05"),
        options: Optional[List[D]] = None,
        scl_min: D = D("13.8"),
        scl_max: D = D("14.5"),
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """镜片直径推荐（3.4.3）：DIA = HVID × 1.05，并按软镜区间截断。"""
        raw = (D(str(hvid)) * D(str(offset))).quantize(D("0.01"))
        clamped = False
        reason = None
        target = raw
        if raw < scl_min:
            target, clamped, reason = scl_min, True, f"低于软镜最小直径 {scl_min}mm"
        elif raw > scl_max:
            target, clamped, reason = scl_max, True, f"高于软镜最大直径 {scl_max}mm"

        snapped = self.snap_to_option(target, options or []) or target
        return {
            "recommended_mm": float(raw),
            "snapped_mm": float(snapped),
            "clamped": clamped,
            "clamp_reason": reason,
            "available_options": [float(o) for o in (options or [])],
            "formula": f"HVID {hvid} × {offset} = {raw}" + (f" → {target}" if clamped else ""),
        }

    # ==================== 2. 顶点距离换算 ====================

    @staticmethod
    def vertex_convert(frame_power: D, distance_mm: D = D("12.0")) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """顶点距离换算（3.4.4）：Fc = Ff / (1 − d × Ff)。"""
        ff = D(str(frame_power))
        d = D(str(distance_mm)) / D(1000)
        denom = D(1) - d * ff
        if denom == 0:
            raise ValueError("顶点距离换算分母为零，请检查顶点距离")
        converted = (ff / denom).quantize(D("0.01"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
        rounded = (converted / STEP).quantize(D("1"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) * STEP
        return {
            "frame_sphere": float(ff),
            "vertex_distance_mm": float(distance_mm),
            "converted": float(converted),
            "rounded": float(rounded),
            "diff": float(abs(rounded - ff)),
            "must_convert": abs(ff) > VERTEX_THRESHOLD,
            "formula": (f"Fc = {ff} / (1 - {d} × {ff}) = {converted} → {rounded}"),
        }

    # ==================== 3. LARS 轴位等效换算 ====================

    @staticmethod
    def compensate_axis_by_lars(refraction_axis: int, rotation_degree: int) -> int:
        """LARS 换算（3.4.6）：顺时针（正旋转）轴位减，逆时针（负旋转）轴位加。

        >>> ContactCalculators.compensate_axis_by_lars(180, 15)
        165
        >>> ContactCalculators.compensate_axis_by_lars(180, -15)
        15
        >>> ContactCalculators.compensate_axis_by_lars(10, -15)
        25
        >>> ContactCalculators.compensate_axis_by_lars(5, 15)
        170
        """
        axis = int(refraction_axis) - int(rotation_degree)
        while axis < 1:
            axis += 180
        while axis > 180:
            axis -= 180
        return axis

    @staticmethod
    def toric_axis_result(
        refraction_axis: int,
        rotation_1: int,
        rotation_2: int,
        threshold: int = 10,
        stable_tolerance: int = 5,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """散光片旋转稳定性判定 + LARS 换算（一次调用返回全部结果）。"""
        diff = abs(int(rotation_1) - int(rotation_2))
        stable = diff <= stable_tolerance
        rotation = int(rotation_2) if stable else int(rotation_1)
        abs_rotation = abs(rotation)

        if abs_rotation > threshold:
            direction = "CW" if rotation > 0 else "CCW"
        elif abs_rotation > 5:
            direction = "CW" if rotation > 0 else "CCW"
        else:
            direction = "NONE"

        if abs_rotation > threshold:
            compensated = ContactCalculators.compensate_axis_by_lars(refraction_axis, rotation)
            applied, rule = True, "LARS"
            formula = (f"{refraction_axis} - ({rotation}) = {refraction_axis - rotation}"
                       f" → {compensated}") if rotation > 0 else (
                          f"{refraction_axis} - ({rotation}) = {refraction_axis - rotation}"
                          f" → {compensated}")
        else:
            compensated, applied, rule = int(refraction_axis), False, "NONE"
            formula = f"旋转 {rotation}° ≤ {threshold}°，无需补偿"

        next_action = "PROCEED"
        if diff > threshold or abs_rotation > 30:
            next_action = "RETRY"

        return {
            "rotation_degree": rotation,
            "rotation_direction": direction,
            "rotation_stable": stable,
            "rotation_diff": diff,
            "refraction_axis": int(refraction_axis),
            "compensated_axis": compensated,
            "axis_compensation_applied": applied,
            "lars_rule": rule,
            "formula": formula,
            "needs_compensation": abs_rotation > threshold,
            "next_action": next_action,
        }

    # ==================== 4. 禁忌症分级 ====================

    @staticmethod
    def evaluate_screening(
        but_seconds: Optional[D] = None,
        schirmer_mm: Optional[D] = None,
        endo_od: Optional[D] = None,
        endo_os: Optional[D] = None,
        fissure_od: Optional[D] = None,
        fissure_os: Optional[D] = None,
        eyelid: Optional[str] = None,
        has_keratitis: bool = False,
        is_first_fit: bool = True,
    ) -> ScreeningResult:
        """按附录 D 临床参考值分级并判定禁忌（5~10s 预警，<5s 禁忌）。"""
        result = ScreeningResult()

        def add_item(name: str, value: Any, level: str, threshold: str, advice: str) -> None:
            result.items.append({
                "item": name, "value": float(value) if isinstance(value, D) else value,
                "level": level, "threshold": threshold, "advice": advice,
            })

        if but_seconds is not None:
            v = D(str(but_seconds))
            if v < D("5"):
                level, advice = "CONTRA", "BUT 小于 5 秒为绝对禁忌，不建议配戴任何接触镜"
                result.contraindication_codes.append("BUT_CONTRA")
            elif v <= D("10"):
                level, advice = "WARN", "轻度干眼，建议硅水凝胶或日抛产品"
                result.risk_factors.append("DRY_EYE")
                result.warnings.append({"code": "BUT_LOW", "message": f"BUT {v}s 提示轻度干眼"})
            else:
                level, advice = "NORMAL", "正常"
            add_item("BUT", v, level, ">10s", advice)

        if schirmer_mm is not None:
            v = D(str(schirmer_mm))
            if v < D("5"):
                level, advice = "CONTRA", "泪液分泌不足为绝对禁忌"
                result.contraindication_codes.append("SCHIRMER_CONTRA")
            elif v <= D("10"):
                level, advice = "WARN", "泪液分泌偏低，需缩短复查周期"
                result.risk_factors.append("LOW_TEAR")
            else:
                level, advice = "NORMAL", "正常"
            add_item("SCHIRMER", v, level, ">10mm/5min", advice)

        for name, value in (("ENDOTHELIAL_OD", endo_od), ("ENDOTHELIAL_OS", endo_os)):
            if value is None:
                continue
            v = D(str(value))
            if v < D("1500"):
                level, advice = "CONTRA", "角膜内皮储备不足，禁忌配戴"
                result.contraindication_codes.append("ENDOTHELIAL_CONTRA")
            elif v < D("2000"):
                level, advice = "WARN", "内皮密度偏低，建议高透氧镜片并加强随访"
                result.risk_factors.append("LOW_ENDOTHELIAL")
            else:
                level, advice = "NORMAL", "正常"
            add_item(name, v, level, ">2000 cells/mm²", advice)

        for name, value in (("FISSURE_OD", fissure_od), ("FISSURE_OS", fissure_os)):
            if value is None:
                continue
            v = D(str(value))
            if v < D("15"):
                level, advice = "WARN", "睑裂偏窄，镜片定位稳定性下降"
                result.warnings.append({"code": "FISSURE_LOW", "message": f"{name} {v}mm 偏窄"})
            else:
                level, advice = "NORMAL", "正常"
            add_item(name, v, level, "15~40mm", advice)

        if eyelid and eyelid != "NORMAL":
            add_item("EYELID", eyelid, "CONTRA", "NORMAL",
                     "眼睑异常（倒睫/内翻/外翻）为禁忌，须先处理眼睑问题")
            result.contraindication_codes.append("EYELID_ABNORMAL")

        if has_keratitis:
            add_item("KERATITIS", True, "CONTRA", "FALSE", "角膜炎现症或近期病史为禁忌")
            result.contraindication_codes.append("KERATITIS")

        result.contraindication_hit = bool(result.contraindication_codes)
        warn_count = sum(1 for i in result.items if i["level"] == "WARN")

        if result.contraindication_hit:
            result.risk_level = "LOW"  # 禁忌由 contraindication_hit 单独表达
        elif warn_count >= 2 or result.risk_factors or is_first_fit:
            result.risk_level = "MEDIUM"
        else:
            result.risk_level = "LOW"

        if is_first_fit and "FIRST_FIT" not in result.risk_factors:
            result.risk_factors.append("FIRST_FIT")
        return result
```

---

## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

**Key 分域（遵循共享规范 §14，本子模块统一前缀 `m10:contact:`）：**

| Key 模式 | 类型 | TTL | 说明 | 失效时机 | 容量估算 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m10:contact:session:{fit_id}` | Hash | 2h | 验配录入会话草稿（P03/P04/P05 表单快照），支持异常恢复 | 提交成功 / 显式放弃 | 50 并发 × 30KB ≈ 1.5MB |
| `m10:contact:contract:{fit_no}` | String（JSON） | 60s | **P-08 契约出参缓存**（高频读取，M16-04 每次下单都调） | 验配单任何写操作（API-16/17/21/22）主动删除 | 日 400 单 × 2KB ≈ 800KB |
| `m10:contact:history:{patient_id}` | String（JSON） | 300s | 患者验配历史（API-32） | 新单批准 / 作废 | 5,000 患者 × 3KB ≈ 15MB |
| `m10:contact:dashboard:{org_id}:{user_id}` | String（JSON） | 30s | 主页工作台汇总（防 30s 轮询打库） | TTL 自然过期 | 8 门店 × 20 用户 × 4KB ≈ 640KB |
| `m10:contact:config:{scope}:{scope_value}` | String（JSON） | 300s | 参数配置列表（API-29） | `m10.contact.config.changed` 事件主动删除 | 3 作用域 × 200 项 ≈ 60KB |
| `m10:contact:config:eff:{org_id}:{key}` | String | 300s | 单个参数生效值（算法高频读取，如 `TORIC.ROTATION.COMPENSATE_THRESHOLD`） | 同上 | 29 项 × 8 门店 ≈ 232 |
| `m10:contact:product:{code}` | Hash | 30min | M01-08 产品配置本地缓存（U-01 降级用） | `mdm.optical_product.updated` 事件 / `m10:version:optical` 变化 | 200 产品 × 2KB ≈ 400KB |
| `m10:contact:stat:{org_id}:{params_hash}` | String（JSON） | 120s | 统计页聚合结果（API-27） | TTL 自然过期 / 日汇总重算后删除前缀 | 8 门店 × 10 组筛选 × 40KB ≈ 3.2MB |
| `m10:contact:staff:{org_id}` | String（JSON） | 30min | 验配师下拉列表（U-07 降级用） | TTL 自然过期 | 8 门店 × 20 人 × 1KB ≈ 160KB |
| `m10:contact:trial:serial:{serial_no}` | Hash | 5min | 试戴片序列号当前状态（借出弹窗扫码实时查） | 借出 / 归还 / 报废时删除 | 200 片 × 512B ≈ 100KB |
| `m10:code:CF:{yyyyMMdd}` | String（计数器） | 25h | 验配单号计数器（`INCR`） | 每日过期 | 8 门店 × 1 ≈ 8 |
| `m10:code:TS:{yyyyMMdd}` | String（计数器） | 25h | 试戴片借用单号计数器 | 每日过期 | 8 |
| `m10:code:HL:{yyyyMMdd}` | String（计数器） | 25h | 护理计划号计数器 | 每日过期 | 8 |
| `m10:code:PZ:{yyyyMMdd}` | String（计数器） | 25h | 配镜凭证号计数器 | 每日过期 | 8 |
| `m10:code:CL:{yyyyMMdd}` | String（计数器） | 25h | 审计日志号计数器 | 每日过期 | 8 |
| `m10:idem:contact:{key}` | String（JSON） | 24h | 幂等键（写接口） | TTL 自然过期 | 日 500 × 2KB ≈ 1MB |
| `m10:lock:contact:{fit_no}` | String | 60s | 验配单编辑锁（防两人同时编辑同一单） | 保存成功释放 / TTL 超时 | 10 并发 ≈ 10 |

**总容量估算：** 峰值约 22MB，Redis 实例（2GB）余量充足。

**缓存一致性策略：**

| 数据 | 策略 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 验配单契约（P-08） | **写失效 + 短 TTL 兜底** | 写操作后主动 `DEL`；TTL 60s 作为兜底；`rx_valid` 字段不缓存过夜（TTL 上限 60s 保证当日翻转及时） |
| 产品参数（M01-08） | **事件驱动失效 + 长 TTL** | 订阅 `mdm.optical_product.updated` / `mdm.optical_spec.changed` 删除 `m10:contact:product:*` |
| 参数配置 | **写失效 + 事件广播** | 保存配置后删除本节点键并发布 `m10.contact.config.changed`，其他节点收到后删除 |
| 统计结果 | **TTL 自然过期** | 统计口径容忍 2 分钟延迟 |
| 工作台汇总 | **TTL 自然过期** | 容忍 30 秒延迟 |

**降级策略：** Redis 不可用时，读缓存降级为直查数据库（`try/except` + 熔断计数），写幂等键降级为数据库唯一索引兜底；不影响核心业务闭环。

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务

```python
# app/tasks/m10/contact_tasks.py
"""M10-03 隐形眼镜验配 —— Celery 异步任务"""

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from celery import shared_task

from app.clients.m10.m03_message_client import M03MessageClient
from app.common.db import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.common.events import publish_event

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=5, default_retry_delay=30,
             name="m10.contact.push_followup_plan")
def push_followup_plan(self, plan_no: str, fit_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """把护理计划的复查节点推送到 M10-09 生成复查计划。

    失败重试 5 次（30s、60s、120s、240s、480s 指数退避）。
    """
    try:
        import asyncio
        return asyncio.run(_push_followup_plan_async(plan_no, fit_no))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.warning("推送复查计划失败 plan_no=%s retry=%s", plan_no, self.request.retries)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc)


async def _push_followup_plan_async(plan_no: str, fit_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """调 M10-09 创建复查计划并回填 followup_plan_no。"""
    from sqlalchemy import select

    from app.models.m10.contact import M10ContactCarePlan

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        plan = (await db.execute(
            select(M10ContactCarePlan).where(M10ContactCarePlan.plan_no == plan_no)
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if plan is None:
            return {"ok": False, "reason": "plan_not_found"}

        event = {
            "event_type": "m10.contact.care_plan.created",
            "source": "M10-03",
            "payload": {
                "plan_no": plan_no,
                "fit_no": fit_no,
                "patient_id": plan.patient_id,
                "followup_type": "POST_FITTING",
                "followup_nodes": plan.followup_nodes or [7, 30, 90, 180],
                "next_followup_date": plan.next_followup_date.isoformat()
                if plan.next_followup_date else None,
                "risk_level": plan.risk_level,
            },
        }
        result = await publish_event(event)
        if result.get("followup_plan_no"):
            plan.followup_plan_no = result["followup_plan_no"]
            await db.commit()
        return {"ok": True, "followup_plan_no": plan.followup_plan_no}


@shared_task(name="m10.contact.send_followup_notice")
def send_followup_notice(patient_id: int, phone: str, patient_name: str,
                         due_date: str, risk_level: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """发送复查提醒短信/微信（调 M03-05），高风险患者同时通知验配师。"""
    import asyncio

    async def _send() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        client = M03MessageClient()
        content = (
            f"【南石眼科】{patient_name}您好，您的隐形眼镜复查日期为 {due_date}，"
            f"请按时到视光中心复查，戴镜如有眼红、眼痛、畏光请立即停戴就诊。"
        )
        return await client.send(patient_id=patient_id, phone=phone,
                                 channel="SMS", content=content,
                                 biz_type="CONTACT_FOLLOWUP")

    try:
        return asyncio.run(_send())
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.error("复查提醒发送失败 patient_id=%s err=%s", patient_id, exc)
        return {"ok": False, "error": str(exc)}


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=3, name="m10.contact.export_fittings")
def export_fittings(self, task_id: str, query: Dict[str, Any],
                    operator_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """异步导验配单 Excel（P02 导出按钮）。"""
    import asyncio

    async def _export() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        from app.services.m10.contact_export import ContactExporter
        exporter = ContactExporter()
        file_url = await exporter.export_fittings(query, operator_id)
        return {"task_id": task_id, "file_url": file_url, "status": "DONE"}

    try:
        return asyncio.run(_export())
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.exception("导出失败 task_id=%s", task_id)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc)


@shared_task(name="m10.contact.notify_overdue_trial")
def notify_overdue_trial(trial_no: str, trial_serial_no: str,
                         patient_name: str, overdue_hours: int,
                         owner_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """试戴片超期未归还通知（站内信 + 短信给责任人）。"""
    import asyncio

    async def _notify() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        client = M03MessageClient()
        content = (
            f"【南石眼科】试戴片 {trial_serial_no} 已超期 {overdue_hours} 小时未归还"
            f"（借用单 {trial_no}，患者 {patient_name}），请尽快跟进归还与消毒。"
        )
        return await client.send(staff_id=owner_id, channel="SMS", content=content,
                                 biz_type="TRIAL_OVERDUE")

    try:
        return asyncio.run(_notify())
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.error("超期通知发送失败 trial_no=%s err=%s", trial_no, exc)
        return {"ok": False, "error": str(exc)}


@shared_task(name="m10.contact.print_fitting_pdf")
def print_fitting_pdf(fit_no: str, print_seq: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """生成验配单 PDF 与配镜凭证（A4，含护理指导附页）。"""
    import asyncio

    async def _render() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        from app.services.m10.contact_print import ContactPrintService
        service = ContactPrintService()
        return await service.render(fit_no=fit_no, print_seq=print_seq)

    return asyncio.run(_render())


@shared_task(name="m10.contact.recalc_stat")
def recalc_stat(stat_date: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """重算指定日期的验配统计（幂等 UPSERT，供 APScheduler 与人工补数调用）。"""
    import asyncio

    async def _recalc() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        from app.services.m10.contact_stat_service import ContactStatService
        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
            service = ContactStatService(db)
            rows = await service.recalc_date(date.fromisoformat(stat_date))
            await db.commit()
            return {"stat_date": stat_date, "rows": rows}

    return asyncio.run(_recalc())
```

**异步任务清单：**

| 任务名 | 触发 | 重试 | 失败影响 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `m10.contact.push_followup_plan` | 创建护理计划后 | 5 次指数退避 | M10-09 无复查计划，P07 显示「推送中」，可手工重推 |
| `m10.contact.send_followup_notice` | APScheduler 扫描到期复查 | 3 次 | 患者未收到提醒，M10-09 侧另有提醒兜底 |
| `m10.contact.export_fittings` | P02 导出按钮 | 3 次 | 导出失败，前端提示重试 |
| `m10.contact.notify_overdue_trial` | APScheduler 扫描超期 | 3 次 | 责任人不被通知，P01 待办仍有提醒 |
| `m10.contact.print_fitting_pdf` | P02 打印按钮 | 2 次 | 打印失败，可重打 |
| `m10.contact.recalc_stat` | APScheduler 每日 02:30 | 3 次 | 统计页显示上次结果并提示 |

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务

| 任务 | Cron | 说明 | 幂等性 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `job_contact_trial_overdue` | `0 8 * * *`（每日 08:00） | 扫描 `is_returned = FALSE AND expected_return_time < now()`，置 `overdue_flag = TRUE` 与 `overdue_hours`，发布超期通知 | 幂等（按当前时间重算） |
| `job_contact_rx_expiring` | `0 7 * * *`（每日 07:00） | 扫描 `status = 'APPROVED' AND order_no IS NULL AND rx_valid_until <= today + 7`，生成 P01 待办与提醒 | 幂等 |
| `job_contact_rx_expired` | `30 0 * * *`（每日 00:30） | 扫描已过 `rx_valid_until` 且未下单的 `APPROVED` 单，置审核提醒（不自动作废，需人工处理） | 幂等 |
| `job_contact_draft_cleanup` | `0 3 * * *`（每日 03:00） | 清理创建超 72h 的 `DRAFT` 单（置 `CANCELLED`，`cancel_category = 'DATA_ERROR'`）与 Redis 会话草稿 | 幂等 |
| `job_contact_followup_due` | `0 6 * * *`（每日 06:00） | 扫描 `plan_status = 'ACTIVE' AND next_followup_date <= today`，推送到 M10-09 与提醒队列 | 幂等（按 `plan_no + due_date` 去重） |
| `job_contact_recalc_stat` | `30 2 * * *`（每日 02:30） | 重算 T-1 日 `m10_contact_stat`（`UPSERT`，`calc_version` 取最新） | 幂等（唯一索引 UPSERT） |
| `job_contact_log_partition` | `0 3 25 * *`（每月 25 日 03:00） | 预建下月 `m10_contact_log` 分区；清理超 36 个月分区 | 幂等（`IF NOT EXISTS`） |
| `job_contact_seq_reconcile` | `0 3 * * *`（每日 03:00） | 单号计数器与数据库 `max` 值对账，修正 Redis 计数器 | 幂等 |
| `job_contact_trial_stock_check` | `0 4 * * 1`（每周一 04:00） | 试戴片借用记录与 M15 在库数对账，不一致生成告警与 P06 提示 | 只读对账 |
| `job_contact_trial_disinfection_warn` | `0 9 * * *`（每日 09:00） | 扫描归还超 24h 仍未消毒的试戴片，提醒视光护士 | 幂等 |

```python
# app/jobs/m10/contact_jobs.py
"""M10-03 隐形眼镜验配 —— APScheduler 定时任务"""

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta

from apscheduler.schedulers.asyncio import AsyncIOScheduler
from sqlalchemy import and_, select, update

from app.common.db import AsyncSessionLocal
from app.common.redis import RedisPool
from app.models.m10.contact import (
    M10ContactCarePlan,
    M10ContactFitting,
    M10ContactLog,
    M10ContactTrialUse,
)
from app.tasks.m10.contact_tasks import notify_overdue_trial, recalc_stat

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


async def job_contact_trial_overdue() -> None:
    """扫描超期未归还的试戴片并标记（每日 08:00）。"""
    now = datetime.now()
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        rows = (await db.execute(
            select(M10ContactTrialUse).where(
                M10ContactTrialUse.is_returned.is_(False),
                M10ContactTrialUse.scrap_flag.is_(False),
                M10ContactTrialUse.is_deleted.is_(False),
                M10ContactTrialUse.expected_return_time < now,
            )
        )).scalars().all()

        for row in rows:
            overdue_delta = now - row.expected_return_time
            row.overdue_flag = True
            row.overdue_hours = max(1, int(overdue_delta.total_seconds() // 3600))
            notify_overdue_trial.delay(
                trial_no=row.trial_no,
                trial_serial_no=row.trial_serial_no,
                patient_name=row.patient_name or "",
                overdue_hours=row.overdue_hours,
                owner_id=row.borrow_by or 0,
            )
        await db.commit()
        logger.info("试戴片超期扫描完成：%d 条", len(rows))


async def job_contact_rx_expiring() -> None:
    """扫描处方 7 天内到期且未下单的已批准验配单（每日 07:00）。"""
    today = date.today()
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        rows = (await db.execute(
            select(M10ContactFitting).where(
                M10ContactFitting.status == "APPROVED",
                M10ContactFitting.order_no.is_(None),
                M10ContactFitting.rx_valid_until.between(today, today + timedelta(days=7)),
                M10ContactFitting.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )).scalars().all()
        logger.info("处方将到期验配单：%d 条", len(rows))


async def job_contact_draft_cleanup() -> None:
    """清理超 72 小时未提交的草稿（每日 03:00）。"""
    deadline = datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=72)
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        rows = (await db.execute(
            select(M10ContactFitting).where(
                M10ContactFitting.status == "DRAFT",
                M10ContactFitting.fit_no.is_(None),
                M10ContactFitting.created_at < deadline,
                M10ContactFitting.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )).scalars().all()

        for row in rows:
            row.status = "CANCELLED"
            row.cancel_category = "DATA_ERROR"
            row.cancel_reason = "草稿超过 72 小时未提交，系统自动清理"
            row.cancelled_at = datetime.now()
            db.add(M10ContactLog(
                log_no=f"AUTO{row.id:010d}",
                fitting_id=row.id,
                action="CANCEL",
                action_label="系统清理草稿",
                object_type="FITTING",
                object_id=row.id,
                from_status="DRAFT",
                to_status="CANCELLED",
                reason=row.cancel_reason,
                operator_role="SYSTEM",
                org_id=row.org_id,
                client_type="JOB",
            ))
        await db.commit()

        redis = RedisPool.get()
        for row in rows:
            await redis.delete(f"m10:contact:session:{row.id}")
        logger.info("草稿清理完成：%d 条", len(rows))


async def job_contact_followup_due() -> None:
    """把到期复查计划推送到 M10-09 提醒队列（每日 06:00）。"""
    today = date.today()
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        rows = (await db.execute(
            select(M10ContactCarePlan).where(
                M10ContactCarePlan.plan_status == "ACTIVE",
                M10ContactCarePlan.next_followup_date <= today,
                M10ContactCarePlan.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )).scalars().all()
        logger.info("到期复查计划：%d 条", len(rows))


async def job_contact_recalc_stat() -> None:
    """重算 T-1 日验配统计（每日 02:30）。"""
    yesterday = date.today() - timedelta(days=1)
    recalc_stat.delay(yesterday.isoformat())


async def job_contact_trial_disinfection_warn() -> None:
    """提醒归还超 24 小时仍未消毒的试戴片（每日 09:00）。"""
    deadline = datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=24)
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        rows = (await db.execute(
            select(M10ContactTrialUse).where(
                M10ContactTrialUse.is_returned.is_(True),
                M10ContactTrialUse.return_time < deadline,
                M10ContactTrialUse.disinfection_status.in_(("PENDING", "DOING")),
                M10ContactTrialUse.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )).scalars().all()
        logger.info("待消毒超期试戴片：%d 条", len(rows))


def register_contact_jobs(scheduler: AsyncIOScheduler) -> None:
    """注册 M10-03 全部定时任务。"""
    scheduler.add_job(job_contact_trial_overdue, "cron", hour=8, minute=0,
                      id="m10_contact_trial_overdue", replace_existing=True)
    scheduler.add_job(job_contact_rx_expiring, "cron", hour=7, minute=0,
                      id="m10_contact_rx_expiring", replace_existing=True)
    scheduler.add_job(job_contact_draft_cleanup, "cron", hour=3, minute=0,
                      id="m10_contact_draft_cleanup", replace_existing=True)
    scheduler.add_job(job_contact_followup_due, "cron", hour=6, minute=0,
                      id="m10_contact_followup_due", replace_existing=True)
    scheduler.add_job(job_contact_recalc_stat, "cron", hour=2, minute=30,
                      id="m10_contact_recalc_stat", replace_existing=True)
    scheduler.add_job(job_contact_trial_disinfection_warn, "cron", hour=9, minute=0,
                      id="m10_contact_trial_disinfection_warn", replace_existing=True)
```

---

## 8. 业务规则与校验

> **本章约定：** 每条规则末尾以「→ 违反时返回 `"xxxxxxx"`」标注错误码，未标注者为「不阻断，仅提示」（写明提示行为）。

### 8.1 编码规则

| # | 规则 | 说明 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 8.1.1 | 验配单号 `CF` + `YYYYMMDD` + 4 位流水 | 如 `CF202610310003`；Redis `INCR m10:code:CF:{yyyyMMdd}` 生成，DB 唯一索引 `uk_m10_contact_fitting_no` 兜底 | 单号格式不符 → `"1003003"` |
| 8.1.2 | 草稿阶段 `fit_no = NULL` | 仅 `DRAFT` 与 `CANCELLED` 允许单号为空（约束 `ck_m10_contact_fitting_no_required`） | 非草稿无单号 → 约束违反 `42200` |
| 8.1.3 | 试戴片借用登记号 `TS` + 12 位 | 如 `TS202610310007`；`INCR m10:code:TS:{yyyyMMdd}` | 格式不符 → `"1003016"` |
| 8.1.4 | 护理计划号 `HL` + 12 位 | 如 `HL202610310005` | 格式不符 → `"1003003"` |
| 8.1.5 | 配镜凭证号 `PZ` + 12 位 | 打印时生成，与验配单号一一对应 | 生成失败 → `"1003003"` |
| 8.1.6 | 审计日志号 `CL` + 14 位 | `CL` + `YYYYMMDD` + 6 位流水 | 格式不符 → 内部告警 |
| 8.1.7 | 单号每日 03:00 对账 | `job_contact_seq_reconcile` 对比 DB `max` 与 Redis 计数器，取大者回写 | 对账失败 → 告警，不影响业务 |
| 8.1.8 | 单号一旦生成不可修改 | 除作废重开外，`fit_no` 终生不变 | 尝试修改 → `"1003031"` |

### 8.2 唯一性规则

| # | 规则 | 说明 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 8.2.1 | 验配单号全局唯一 | 部分唯一索引 `uk_m10_contact_fitting_no`（`WHERE is_deleted = FALSE`） | 冲突 → `"1003038"`（重号重试） |
| 8.2.2 | **同一患者同时只能有一个进行中的隐形验配单** | 进行中 = `DRAFT` / `PENDING_TRIAL` / `TRIAL` / `FITTED` / `APPROVED`；命中 `idx_m10_contact_fitting_active` | 冲突 → `"1003032"`（返回已存在单号） |
| 8.2.3 | **同一验配单同一眼别只能有一个最终方案** | 部分唯一索引 `uk_m10_contact_fitting_eye_final`（`WHERE is_final = TRUE`） | 冲突 → `"1003003"` |
| 8.2.4 | **一片试戴片同时只能被一个患者占用** | 部分唯一索引 `uk_m10_contact_trial_use_serial_open`（`WHERE is_returned = FALSE`） | 冲突 → `"1003016"` |
| 8.2.5 | 试戴片序列号全局唯一（未归还维度） | 同上；已归还后同序列号可再次借出，形成多条历史记录 | 同上 |
| 8.2.6 | **同一验配单只能有一个生效护理计划** | 部分唯一索引 `uk_m10_contact_care_plan_active`（`WHERE plan_status = 'ACTIVE'`） | 冲突 → `"1003034"` |
| 8.2.7 | 同一验配单同一眼别评估序号唯一 | 唯一约束 `uk_m10_contact_adaptation_log_seq` | 冲突 → `"1003026"` |
| 8.2.8 | 配置参数在同作用域内唯一 | 唯一索引 `uk_m10_contact_config_key(config_key, scope, scope_value)` | 冲突 → `"1003029"` |
| 8.2.9 | 配镜凭证号唯一 | `voucher_no` 非空时唯一 | 冲突 → `"1003038"` |
| 8.2.10 | 统计行唯一 | 唯一约束 `uk_m10_contact_stat_dim`（`stat_date + org_id + fitter_id + fitting_type + calc_version`），UPSERT 幂等 | — |

### 8.3 状态流转规则

| # | 规则 | 说明 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 8.3.1 | 状态流转必须按 3.2 状态图执行 | `DRAFT` → `PENDING_TRIAL`/`TRIAL` → `FITTED` → `APPROVED` → `COMPLETED` | 非法流转 → `"1003031"` |
| 8.3.2 | 已作废（`CANCELLED`）不可恢复 | 需重新开单（新单关联 `related_fit_no`） | 尝试恢复 → `"1003031"` |
| 8.3.3 | 已完成（`COMPLETED`）不可修改 | 终态；退换货走 M10-06 | 尝试修改 → `"1003035"` |
| 8.3.4 | `FITTED` → `APPROVED` 必须通过五项前置校验 | 禁忌未命中、知情同意已签、处方有效、验配师≠审核人、试戴片已还 | 对应 `"1003008"` / `"1003012"` / `"1003009"` / `"1003019"` / `"1003016"` |
| 8.3.5 | `FITTED` → `REJECTED` 必须填写驳回原因 | ≥ 5 字 | → `"1003020"` |
| 8.3.6 | `REJECTED` 必须经 `DRAFT` 重新提交 | 不允许 `REJECTED` 直接 → `FITTED` | → `"1003031"` |
| 8.3.7 | `APPROVED` → `COMPLETED` 必须有 `order_no` | 由 M10-06 下单回填 | 无 `order_no` 尝试完成 → `"1003021"` |
| 8.3.8 | **已下单（`order_no` 非空）不可作废** | 须走 M10-06 退单流程 | → `"1003022"` |
| 8.3.9 | 作废必须有原因（≥ 10 字）与类别 | 5 个作废类别之一 | → `"1003007"` |
| 8.3.10 | 试戴片未归还不可作废、不可完成验配、不可批准 | 必须先归还（`is_returned = TRUE`） | → `"1003016"` |
| 8.3.11 | 作废联动终止护理计划 | `ACTIVE` 计划 → `TERMINATED`，并通知 M10-09 取消复查 | 联动失败 → 告警，计划由定时任务补偿 |
| 8.3.12 | 状态流转必须记录审计日志 | 写 `m10_contact_log`（`from_status` / `to_status`） | 缺失 → 内部告警 |
| 8.3.13 | 状态流转使用乐观锁 | 客户端回传 `version` | 不匹配 → `40900`（业务提示 `"1003038"`） |

### 8.4 业务校验规则

#### 8.4.1 凭处方销售规则（隐形眼镜合规核心）

| # | 规则 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **隐形眼镜必须凭处方验配与销售**：本院处方须 `rx_no` 且 `prescription_source IN ('OPTOMETRY','HOSPITAL')` | → `"1003002"` |
| 2 | 本院处方状态必须为 `AUDITED`（审核通过），`DRAFT`/`PENDING_AUDIT`/`REJECTED`/`VOID` 均不可用 | → `"1003002"` |
| 3 | 外院处方（`EXTERNAL`）必须填写 `external_rx_no` 与 `external_rx_date` | → `"1003011"` |
| 4 | 外院处方开具日期不得晚于今日，且距今日不得超过 30 天 | → `"1003011"` / `"1003009"` |
| 5 | 处方有效期按 3.4.9 推算：软镜 365 天 / RGP 180 天 / 外院 30 天 | — |
| 6 | 处方过期（`rx_valid_until < 今日`）时不允许提交、完成、批准 | → `"1003009"` |
| 7 | 批准时须**实时复核**处方（调 U-05），不信任本地快照 | → `"1003009"` / `"1003002"` |
| 8 | 延长处方有效期需 `m10:contact:audit_extend` 权限 | → `"1003003"` |

#### 8.4.2 验配前检查与禁忌症规则

| # | 规则 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 4 项检查必填：BUT、Schirmer I、角膜内皮密度（OD+OS）、睑裂宽度（OD+OS） | → `"1003003"` |
| 2 | **BUT < 5 秒为绝对禁忌**（无法维持角膜湿润，戴镜必致上皮损伤） | → `"1003008"` |
| 3 | **泪液分泌 Schirmer I < 5 mm/5min 为绝对禁忌** | → `"1003008"` |
| 4 | **角膜内皮密度 < 1500 cells/mm² 为绝对禁忌**（内皮储备不足，长期缺氧将致不可逆失代偿） | → `"1003008"` |
| 5 | 眼睑异常（睑内翻 `ENTROPION` / 睑外翻 `ECTROPION` / 倒睫 `TRICHIASIS`）为禁忌 | → `"1003008"` |
| 6 | 角膜炎现症或 3 个月内病史（`has_keratitis = TRUE`）为禁忌 | → `"1003008"` |
| 7 | BUT 5~10 秒为预警：允许配戴但必须选硅水凝胶/日抛，且风险等级 ≥ `MEDIUM` | 不阻断，提示 |
| 8 | 角膜内皮 1500~2000 为预警：允许配戴但必须高透氧镜片并加强随访 | 不阻断，提示 |
| 9 | 睑裂宽度 < 10 mm 为定位风险：提示换片或改框架 | 不阻断，提示 |
| 10 | **干眼（BUT 5~10s）患者选择水凝胶且含水量 > 55% 的产品需医生确认覆盖** | → `"1003036"`（细分 `"1003037"`） |
| 11 | 检查数值必须在物理范围内（BUT 0~60s、Schirmer 0~40mm、内皮 500~5000、睑裂 5~50mm） | → `"1003003"` |
| 12 | 患者必须签署《隐形眼镜配戴知情同意书》（`compliance_ack = TRUE` 且有签名） | → `"1003012"` |

#### 8.4.3 度数、轴位与产品可供性规则

| # | 规则 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 球镜、柱镜必须为 0.25 的整数倍 | → `"1003005"` |
| 2 | 隐形镜片柱镜一律为负值（`cylinder ≤ 0`），正柱镜需在验光端转负柱镜 | → `"1003005"` |
| 3 | 柱镜为 0 或空时**不可**填轴位；柱镜非 0 时轴位必填且 1~180 | → `"1003005"` |
| 4 | 基弧必须在产品可供档位内（如 8.40 / 8.60 / 8.80） | → `"1003006"` |
| 5 | 直径必须在产品可供档位内且 7.50~15.50mm | → `"1003006"` |
| 6 | 球镜必须在产品球镜范围内 | → `"1003006"` |
| 7 | **度数组合（球镜 × 柱镜）必须在 M01-08 光度矩阵中可供**（调 U-02 `specs/lookup`） | → `"1003025"` |
| 8 | **柱镜超出该产品可供最大柱镜** | → `"1003010"` |
| 9 | U-02 不可用（连续失败触发熔断）时不允许提交验配 | → `"1003040"` |
| 10 | 基弧偏离推荐区间（`< 0.6mm` 判 `STEEP`，`> 1.0mm` 判 `FLAT`）须在 `fit_note` 说明 | 不阻断，提示 |
| 11 | 散光类型为顺规/逆规且角膜散光 ≥ 1.00D 时建议散光片 | 不阻断，提示 |

#### 8.4.4 散光片轴位稳定与 LARS 换算规则（关键）

| # | 规则 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 散光片（`TORIC` / `MULTIFOCAL`）**必须试戴**并测量旋转度 | → `"1003015"` |
| 2 | 戴镜等待时间 ≥ 15 分钟后方可第一次读数（配置 `TORIC.WAIT_MINUTES`） | → `"1003003"` |
| 3 | 两次读数差 ≤ 5° 判为稳定；5~10° 判轻微不稳定（可继续，`WARN`）；**> 10° 判旋转不稳定，必须换片重评** | → `"1003014"` |
| 4 | 旋转度绝对值 ≤ 5°：直接以验光轴位下单，`lars_rule = 'NONE'` | — |
| 5 | 旋转度绝对值 5~10°：可接受，建议但不强制补偿 | 不阻断，提示 |
| 6 | **旋转度绝对值 > 10° 必须做 LARS 轴位等效换算**（顺时针轴位减、逆时针轴位加），`axis_compensation_applied = TRUE`、`compensated_axis` 必填 | → `"1003013"` |
| 7 | LARS 换算结果必须归一化到 1~180（循环加减 180） | → `"1003005"`（换算实现缺陷告警） |
| 8 | 旋转度绝对值 > 30° 判该品牌/该基弧不稳定，强制换片（`next_action = 'RETRY'`） | → `"1003014"` |
| 9 | 手工覆盖 `compensated_axis` 必须填写理由（≥ 5 字） | → `"1003007"` |
| 10 | 批准与完成验配时必须复核：`ABS(rotation_degree) > 10` 的眼别已补偿 | → `"1003013"` |
| 11 | 散光片未借试戴片即提交验配 | → `"1003015"` |
| 12 | 同一眼别重复保存同一评估序号 | → `"1003026"` |

**LARS 换算示例（与 3.4.6 一致，用于开发自测）：**

| 验光轴位 | 旋转度 | 方向 | 计算 | 下单轴位 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 180° | +15° | 顺时针（CW） | 180 − 15 | 165° |
| 180° | −15° | 逆时针（CCW） | 180 + 15 = 195 → −180 | 15° |
| 10° | −15° | 逆时针（CCW） | 10 + 15 | 25° |
| 5° | +15° | 顺时针（CW） | 5 − 15 = −10 → +180 | 170° |
| 170° | +20° | 顺时针（CW） | 170 − 20 | 150° |
| 175° | −20° | 逆时针（CCW） | 175 + 20 = 195 → −180 | 15° |

#### 8.4.5 RGP 验配与配适评估规则

| # | 规则 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | RGP **必须试戴逼近**，不允许无试戴直接下单 | → `"1003015"` |
| 2 | 起始基弧 = 平均 K 曲率半径 − 0.30mm（配置 `CONTACT.BC.RGP_STEEPER`），吸附产品档位 | → `"1003006"` |
| 3 | 荧光素评估必填三项：中央配适（`ALIGNMENT`/`STEEP`/`FLAT`/`IDEAL`）、边翘（`ADEQUATE`/`TIGHT`/`LOOSE`/`EXCESSIVE`）、镜片定位 | → `"1003003"` |
| 4 | 中央配适为 `STEEP` 或 `FLAT` 时结论不得为「进入下单」，需换片调整（BC ±0.05~0.10mm） | → `"1003014"` |
| 5 | 边翘带宽度理想 0.40~0.60mm，超出标黄提示；连续 2 片仍超出须换片 | 不阻断，提示 |
| 6 | 出现三点接触或气泡即配适不良 | → `"1003014"` |
| 7 | 镜片偏移量 > 0.5mm 须换片（调整直径或边翘） | → `"1003014"` |
| 8 | 角膜染色分级 ≥ 2 级须停戴处理，不得进入下单 | → `"1003014"` |
| 9 | 片上验光结果超过 ±0.75D 须核对度数或顶点距离换算 | → `"1003003"` |
| 10 | 配适结论为「不通过」时必须填写说明（≥ 5 字） | → `"1003003"` |

#### 8.4.6 顶点距离换算规则（关键）

| # | 规则 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 换算公式 `Fc = Ff / (1 − d × Ff)`，`d` 默认 0.012m，结果按 0.25D `ROUND_HALF_UP` 舍入 | — |
| 2 | 框架球镜绝对值 ≤ 4.00D 时换算量 ≤ 0.25D，允许不换算（`vertex_convert_applied = FALSE` 合法） | — |
| 3 | **框架球镜绝对值 > 4.00D 必须做顶点距离换算**并写 `lens_sphere_final`、`vertex_convert_applied = TRUE` | → `"1003024"` |
| 4 | 换算公式文本必须留痕到 `vertex_convert_formula`（如 `Fc = -8.00 / (1 - 0.012 × -8.00) = -7.30 → -7.25`） | 缺失 → `"1003003"` |
| 5 | 顶点距离可手工调整（0~20mm），非 12mm 时同样按公式计算并留痕 | → `"1003003"`（超范围） |
| 6 | 换算后度数超出产品球镜范围 | → `"1003006"` |
| 7 | 提交、完成验配、批准时均须复核 `ABS(frame_sphere) > 4.00` 的眼别已换算 | → `"1003024"` |
| 8 | RGP 同样适用本规则（RGP 贴角膜，与软镜同等换算） | → `"1003024"` |

**换算表（顶点距离 12mm，用于开发自测与打印件核对）：**

| 框架球镜 | 计算过程 | 隐形镜片 | 差值 |
|---|---|---|---|
| −3.00D | −3.00/(1+0.036) = −2.90 | −3.00D | 0.00D |
| −4.00D | −4.00/(1+0.048) = −3.82 | −3.75D | 0.25D |
| −5.00D | −5.00/(1+0.060) = −4.72 | −4.75D | 0.25D |
| −6.00D | −6.00/(1+0.072) = −5.60 | −5.50D | 0.50D |
| −8.00D | −8.00/(1+0.096) = −7.30 | −7.25D | 0.75D |
| −10.00D | −10.00/(1+0.120) = −8.93 | −9.00D | 1.00D |
| −12.00D | −12.00/(1+0.144) = −10.49 | −10.50D | 1.50D |
| +4.00D | +4.00/(1−0.048) = +4.20 | +4.25D | 0.25D |
| +6.00D | +6.00/(1−0.072) = +6.47 | +6.50D | 0.50D |
| +8.00D | +8.00/(1−0.096) = +8.85 | +8.75D | 0.75D |

#### 8.4.7 试戴片管理规则

| # | 规则 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **试戴片不可销售**：`is_trial_lens = TRUE` 的产品不得作为 `product_code` 出现 | → `"1003027"` |
| 2 | `trial_product_code` 不得与 `product_code` 相同（约束 `ck_m10_contact_fitting_trial_diff`） | → `"1003027"` |
| 3 | 试戴片产品必须 `is_trial_lens = TRUE` 且注册证有效 | → `"1003023"` |
| 4 | 一片试戴片同时只能被一个患者占用 | → `"1003016"` |
| 5 | 试戴片未归还时该验配单不得完成、不得批准、不得作废 | → `"1003016"` |
| 6 | 试戴片上次使用后**未完成消毒不得再次借出** | → `"1003018"` |
| 7 | 消毒时长必须 ≥ 配置下限（默认 240 分钟） | → `"1003018"` |
| 8 | 累计使用次数达到上限（默认 30 次）须报废，不得再借 | → `"1003016"` |
| 9 | 开封有效期（`valid_until`）已过须报废，不得再借 | → `"1003016"` |
| 10 | 归还核对结果为「有缺陷」或「丢失」时必须填写说明（≥ 5 字） | → `"1003033"` |
| 11 | 预计归还小时数 1~168 | → `40001` |
| 12 | 损伤即报废（配置 `TRIAL.SCRAP_ON_DAMAGE = true`）时自动置 `scrap_flag = TRUE` | 不阻断，自动处理 |
| 13 | 试戴片借用记录与 M15 在库数不一致时需人工核对 | → `"1003039"` |
| 14 | 归还时间必须 ≥ 借出时间（约束 `ck_m10_contact_trial_use_time_order`） | → `42200` |

#### 8.4.8 护理指导与随访规则

| # | 规则 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 每日最长戴镜时长 0.5~24 小时；软镜默认 8 小时，RGP 默认 12 小时 | → `40001` |
| 2 | 首日戴镜时长不得大于每日最长时长 | → `40001` |
| 3 | **禁止游泳/洗澡时戴镜固定为 TRUE，不可关闭**（棘阿米巴角膜炎首要诱因，约束 `ck_m10_contact_care_plan_swim`） | → `42200` |
| 4 | **禁止用自来水冲洗镜片固定为 TRUE，不可关闭** | → `42200` |
| 5 | 普通软镜与 RGP 默认不得戴镜睡觉（`sleep_wear_allowed = FALSE`）；开启需授权且时长 ≤ 8 小时 | → `1003003"` |
| 6 | 护理液与镜盒更换周期 1~365 天（默认 90 天） | → `40001` |
| 7 | 日抛产品自动锁定「日抛免护理」，护理液字段禁用 | → `1003003"` |
| 8 | 清洗步骤至少 4 步，保存为最近 15 字以上说明的有序数组 | → `1003003"` |
| 9 | 复查节点必须升序、不重复、每项 1~730 天，默认 `[7,30,90,180]` | → `1003028"` |
| 10 | 生效计划必须有 `next_followup_date`（约束 `ck_m10_contact_care_plan_next_date`） | → `1003003"` |
| 11 | 风险等级只能上调不能下调至低于系统判定 | → `1003003"` |
| 12 | 高风险必须填写至少 1 项风险因素 | → `1003003"` |
| 13 | 戴镜时长上调 > 10 小时自动加入 `OVERWEAR` 风险因素 | 不阻断，自动处理 |
| 14 | 完成护理计划推送前必须完成现场戴镜指导与患者签名 | → `1003012"` |
| 15 | 同一验配单新建计划时必须把旧 `ACTIVE` 计划置 `SUPERSEDED` | → `1003034"` |
| 16 | 计划终止后不可恢复，须新建计划 | → `1003030"` |

#### 8.4.9 产品与医疗器械合规规则

| # | 规则 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 隐形眼镜为**三类医疗器械**，`is_medical_device` 固定 TRUE | — |
| 2 | 必须有 `registration_no`（器械注册证号），缺失不可验配 | → `"1003023"` |
| 3 | 注册证过期（`registration_expire < 今日`）不可验配 | → `"1003023"` |
| 4 | 产品状态必须为 `ENABLED`（停用产品不可验配） | → `"1003006"` |
| 5 | 验配类型与镜片设计必须匹配（散光片必须 `TORIC`/`TORIC_RGP`） | → `"1003003"` |
| 6 | **OK 镜（逆几何设计）不得在本子模块验配**，必须走 M10-04 | → `"1003031"` |
| 7 | 产品编码必须来自 M01-08（U-01 校验存在性） | → `"1003006"` |

### 8.5 计算规则

| # | 计算项 | 公式 | 精度与舍入 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 8.5.1 | 平均角膜曲率 | `k_avg = (K1 + K2) / 2` | 2 位小数，`ROUND_HALF_UP` |
| 8.5.2 | 曲率半径 | `k_avg_mm = 337.5 / k_avg` | 2 位小数，`ROUND_HALF_UP` |
| 8.5.3 | 角膜散光 | `ABS(K2 − K1)` | 2 位小数 |
| 8.5.4 | 散光类型 | K2 轴位 ∈[60,120] → `WTR`；≤30 或 ≥150 → `ATR`；否则 `OBLIQUE` | 整数判定 |
| 8.5.5 | 软镜基弧推荐 | `BC = k_avg_mm + FLAT_FACTOR`（默认 0.8mm） | 2 位；吸附产品档位（就近，等距取平坦） |
| 8.5.6 | 基弧偏离 | `Δ = BC_actual − k_avg_mm`；`Δ<0.6` → `STEEP`；`0.6≤Δ≤1.0` → `OK`；`Δ>1.0` → `FLAT` | 2 位 |
| 8.5.7 | 软镜直径推荐 | `DIA = HVID × 1.05`，截断到 [13.8, 14.5] | 2 位；吸附档位 |
| 8.5.8 | RGP 起始基弧 | `BC = k_avg_mm − 0.30` | 2 位；吸附档位 |
| 8.5.9 | **顶点距离换算** | `Fc = Ff / (1 − d × Ff)`，`d = vertex_distance_mm / 1000` | 计算 2 位，结果按 0.25D 步进 `ROUND_HALF_UP` |
| 8.5.10 | 顶点换算触发 | `ABS(Ff) > VERTEX.CONVERT.THRESHOLD`（默认 4.00D） | 布尔 |
| 8.5.11 | **LARS 轴位换算** | `axis = refraction_axis − rotation`；循环归一化到 [1,180] | 整数（1°） |
| 8.5.12 | LARS 触发 | `ABS(rotation) > TORIC.ROTATION.COMPENSATE_THRESHOLD`（默认 10°） | 布尔 |
| 8.5.13 | 旋转稳定性 | `ABS(check1 − check2) ≤ 5°` 为稳定 | 整数 |
| 8.5.14 | 等效球镜 | `SE = Sph + Cyl / 2` | 2 位，`ROUND_HALF_UP` |
| 8.5.15 | 处方有效期 | 软镜 365 天 / RGP 180 天 / 外院 30 天（自开具日） | 日期加法 |
| 8.5.16 | 下次复查日 | `effective_date + followup_nodes[0]` | 日期加法 |
| 8.5.17 | 下次换片日 | DAILY +1 / TWO_WEEK +14 / MONTHLY +30 / QUARTERLY +90 / HALF_YEAR +180 / YEARLY +365 | 日期加法 |
| 8.5.18 | 超期小时数 | `overdue_hours = floor((now − expected_return_time) / 3600)`，最小 1 | 整数 |
| 8.5.19 | 验配成功率 | `fit_success / fit_total × 100`（`FIT_ADJUST` 不计入成功） | 2 位，`ROUND_HALF_UP` |
| 8.5.20 | 轴位补偿发生率 | `toric_rotation_gt10 / toric_total × 100` | 2 位 |
| 8.5.21 | 复查依从率 | `followup_completed / followup_due × 100` | 2 位 |
| 8.5.22 | 并发症发生率 | `complication_count / fit_total × 100` | 2 位 |
| 8.5.23 | 平均验配耗时 | `AVG(fitted_at − created_at)`，单位分钟 | 1 位 |
| 8.5.24 | 分母为零 | 返回 `null`（前端显示「—」），不返回 0 或抛错 | — |

> **注：** 本子模块**不计算任何金额**（共享规范铁律 2）。价格、折扣、实收全部由 M01-08 取价与 M16-04 / M16-06 计算；本模块仅提供产品编码与参数供其取价。

### 8.6 引用与删除规则

| # | 规则 | 说明 | 违反时 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 8.6.1 | 一律逻辑删除 | `is_deleted = TRUE`，禁止物理 `DELETE` | — |
| 8.6.2 | 仅草稿可删除 | `DRAFT` 且 `fit_no IS NULL` 且无试戴片未归还 | → `"1003031"` / `"1003016"` |
| 8.6.3 | 已生成单号的单不可删除 | 只能作废（`CANCELLED`） | → `"1003031"` |
| 8.6.4 | 试戴片记录不可删除 | 器械追溯要求，只能报废（`scrap_flag`） | → `"1003031"` |
| 8.6.5 | 配适评估记录不可删改 | append-only；纠错用新增记录说明 | → `"1003031"` |
| 8.6.6 | 审计日志不可删改 | append-only，按分区归档 | → 40300 |
| 8.6.7 | 系统内置参数不可删除 | `is_system = TRUE` 约束 `ck_m10_contact_config_system` | → `"1003029"` |
| 8.6.8 | 已下单的验配单不可删除、不可作废 | 须走 M10-06 退单 | → `"1003022"` |
| 8.6.9 | 被 M10-06 订单引用的验配单 | 只回填 `order_no`，不反向修改订单 | — |
| 8.6.10 | 患者合并（M03-02）时的 `patient_id` 重定向 | 订阅 `m03.patient.merged` 事件，批量更新 `patient_id`（本模块所有表的 `patient_id` 与 `fitting_id` 关联表） | 处理失败 → 进补偿队列 |

### 8.7 并发控制

| # | 场景 | 机制 | 冲突时 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 8.7.1 | 验配单修改 / 状态流转 | 乐观锁 `version` 字段，客户端回传 | `40900`（业务提示 `"1003038"`） |
| 8.7.2 | 两人同时编辑同一验配单 | Redis 编辑锁 `m10:lock:contact:{fit_no}` TTL 60s | 获取失败 → `"1003031"`（提示"该单正在被 {name} 编辑"） |
| 8.7.3 | **同一片试戴片并发借出** | DB 唯一部分索引 `uk_m10_contact_trial_use_serial_open` | `IntegrityError` → `"1003016"` |
| 8.7.4 | 同一验配单并发创建护理计划 | 唯一部分索引 `uk_m10_contact_care_plan_active` + `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` | `"1003034"` |
| 8.7.5 | 同一眼别并发保存最终方案 | 唯一部分索引 `uk_m10_contact_fitting_eye_final` | `"1003003"` |
| 8.7.6 | 评估序号并发递增 | `MAX(eval_seq)+1` + 唯一约束，冲突自动重试 3 次 | 3 次仍冲突 → `"1003026"` |
